 > **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
 
 **Goal:** Expor o editor inteiro por MCP HTTP local, com portas automáticas e um conector stdio configurado uma única vez que vincula cada conversa ao editor escolhido.
 
 **Architecture:** Cada editor mantém seu servidor e sua identidade. O conector descobre instâncias e encaminha ferramentas com `binding_id` explícito, sem destino global. Operações MCP e UI compartilham serviços de domínio; snapshots, revisões, histórico e jobs preservam o estado vivo.
 
 **Tech Stack:** C#, SDK .NET `9.0.308`, alvos `net9.0`/`net9.0-windows` existentes, System.Text.Json, xUnit `2.9.3`, Microsoft.NET.Test.Sdk `17.14.1`. Proposta para revisão: MCP C# SDK `2.2.0`, Kestrel embutido e HTTP stateless; validar na tarefa 01 antes de expandir a implementação.

 ## Global Constraints
 
 - “Cada instância do editor hospeda seu próprio servidor MCP HTTP.”
 - “O acesso é local, em `127.0.0.1`.” Bind na porta `0`; publicar o endereço efetivamente atribuído.
 - “Não existe variável global de ‘editor atual’” é a regra operacional: todo acesso a editor exige vínculo explícito.
 - “Editar uma cena não a salva automaticamente.” Salvamento e descarte precisam de intenção explícita.
 - “O histórico é compartilhado com a interface e com outros clientes; não há uma pilha privada de undo por conversa.”
 - “A entrega cobre o editor inteiro.” As tarefas são etapas, não redução de escopo.
 - Manter SDK `9.0.308` e os alvos atuais; não atualizar runtime para acomodar dependência sem feedback de Helena.

+namespace helengine.editor.automation;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Holds bounded resource limits shared by editor automation hosts and their tests.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class EditorAutomationLimits
+{
+    /// <summary>Creates limits with the contract's production-safe defaults.</summary>
+    public EditorAutomationLimits() : this(100, 500, 1024, 64, 10000, 4 * 1024 * 1024, 10000)
+    {
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Creates injectable positive limits for deterministic boundary tests.</summary>
+    /// <param name="DefaultPageSize">Number of records requested when a caller omits a page size.</param>
+    /// <param name="MaximumPageSize">Largest page a caller may request.</param>
+    /// <param name="MaximumPendingCalls">Maximum concurrent requests admitted by an instance.</param>
+    /// <param name="MaximumBindings">Maximum live client bindings in one editor instance.</param>
+    /// <param name="MaximumOperationIdsPerBinding">Maximum retained mutation identities per binding.</param>
+    /// <param name="MaximumRequestBytes">Maximum accepted request body size.</param>
+    /// <param name="MaximumRetainedLogEntries">Maximum log entries retained by an instance.</param>
+    public EditorAutomationLimits(int DefaultPageSize, int MaximumPageSize, int MaximumPendingCalls, int MaximumBindings, int MaximumOperationIdsPerBinding, int MaximumRequestBytes, int MaximumRetainedLogEntries)
+    {
+        if (DefaultPageSize <= 0 || MaximumPageSize < DefaultPageSize || MaximumPendingCalls <= 0 || MaximumBindings <= 0 || MaximumOperationIdsPerBinding <= 0 || MaximumRequestBytes <= 0 || MaximumRetainedLogEntries <= 0)
+        {
+            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(DefaultPageSize), "Every limit must be positive and the maximum page must include the default page size.");
+        }
+
+        this.DefaultPageSize = DefaultPageSize;
+        this.MaximumPageSize = MaximumPageSize;
+        this.MaximumPendingCalls = MaximumPendingCalls;
+        this.MaximumBindings = MaximumBindings;
+        this.MaximumOperationIdsPerBinding = MaximumOperationIdsPerBinding;
+        this.MaximumRequestBytes = MaximumRequestBytes;
+        this.MaximumRetainedLogEntries = MaximumRetainedLogEntries;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the page size used when a caller does not supply a limit.</summary>
+    public int DefaultPageSize { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the upper bound applied to a requested page size.</summary>
+    public int MaximumPageSize { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the maximum number of accepted but unfinished calls.</summary>
+    public int MaximumPendingCalls { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the maximum number of active editor bindings.</summary>
+    public int MaximumBindings { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the maximum mutation idempotency records retained for one binding.</summary>
+    public int MaximumOperationIdsPerBinding { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the maximum size, in bytes, of a request body.</summary>
+    public int MaximumRequestBytes { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets the maximum count of log records retained for pagination.</summary>
+    public int MaximumRetainedLogEntries { get; }
+}

+using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
+
+namespace helengine.editor.automation;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Represents a client's explicit association with one verified editor instance.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class EditorBindingDescriptor
+{
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the random binding identity, independent of the endpoint port.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("binding_id")]
+    public string BindingId { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the full identity snapshot of the bound editor instance.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("instance")]
+    public EditorInstanceDescriptor Instance { get; set; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the binding lifecycle state reported to the client.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("state")]
+    public string State { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+}

+using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
+
+namespace helengine.editor.automation;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Identifies a verified running editor instance without exposing transport credentials.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class EditorInstanceDescriptor
+{
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the stable random identity of the editor process instance.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("instance_id")]
+    public string InstanceId { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the displayed project name.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("project_name")]
+    public string ProjectName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the canonical root path of the opened project.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("project_root_path")]
+    public string ProjectRootPath { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the identity of the current project generation.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("project_generation")]
+    public string ProjectGeneration { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the local MCP endpoint currently published by the editor.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("endpoint")]
+    public string Endpoint { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the version of the Helengine automation contract.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("contract_version")]
+    public int ContractVersion { get; set; } = 1;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the displayed editor version.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("editor_version")]
+    public string EditorVersion { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the operating-system process id as an integer.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("process_id")]
+    public int ProcessId { get; set; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the UTC-aware time at which the editor instance started.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("started_at")]
+    public DateTimeOffset StartedAt { get; set; }
+}

+using System.Text.Json;
+using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
+
+namespace helengine.editor.automation;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Captures an observable asynchronous editor job state and its detached result payload.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class EditorJobSnapshot
+{
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the stable job identity.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("job_id")]
+    public string JobId { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the operation family or job kind.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("kind")]
+    public string Kind { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets a state from queued, running, cancel_requested, cancelled, succeeded, or failed.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("state")]
+    public string State { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets a concise status message suitable for a client.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("message")]
+    public string Message { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets whether a cancellation request is currently valid.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("can_cancel")]
+    public bool CanCancel { get; set; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets structured progress values detached from editor objects.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("progress")]
+    public JsonElement Progress { get; set; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the final detached result, if the job has completed.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("result")]
+    public JsonElement Result { get; set; }
+}

+using System.Text.Json;
+using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
+
+namespace helengine.editor.automation;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Carries one validated editor-tool invocation across the shared automation boundary.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class EditorOperationRequest
+{
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the exact catalog name of the requested operation.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("tool_name")]
+    public string ToolName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the explicit editor binding that owns this request.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("binding_id")]
+    public string BindingId { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the live scene identity when the operation addresses a scene.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("scene_handle")]
+    public string SceneHandle { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the revision that a mutation must match before it can execute.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("expected_revision")]
+    public string ExpectedRevision { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the caller-generated idempotency identity for a mutation.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("operation_id")]
+    public string OperationId { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the detached JSON object containing tool-specific arguments.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("arguments")]
+    public JsonElement Arguments { get; set; }
+}

+using System.Text.Json;
+using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
+
+namespace helengine.editor.automation;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Describes the confirmed outcome or explicit failure of one editor operation.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class EditorOperationResult
+{
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets whether the editor completed the requested operation.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("succeeded")]
+    public bool Succeeded { get; set; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the stable machine-readable error code, or an empty string on success.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("error_code")]
+    public string ErrorCode { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets a human-readable explanation of the outcome.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("message")]
+    public string Message { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the identity of the editor instance that handled the request.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("instance_id")]
+    public string InstanceId { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the project generation observed while handling the request.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("project_generation")]
+    public string ProjectGeneration { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the resulting project revision when one is known.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("revision")]
+    public string Revision { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the operation identity echoed by the editor.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("operation_id")]
+    public string OperationId { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets detached JSON data that identifies returned values and applied effects.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("data")]
+    public JsonElement Data { get; set; }
+}

new file mode 100644
index 00000000..8c53cac6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/engine/helengine.editor.automation/EditorToolCatalog.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,96 @@
+using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
+using System.Reflection;
+using System.Text.Json;
+using Json.Schema;
+
+namespace helengine.editor.automation;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Loads the immutable editor tool contract and applies its JSON Schema rules to runtime arguments.
+/// </summary>
+public static class EditorToolCatalog
+{
+    /// <summary>Stores every compiled input schema by the exact public tool name.</summary>
+    static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, JsonSchema> InputSchemas;
+
+    /// <summary>Stores the declarative catalog in its original stable ordering.</summary>
+    static readonly IReadOnlyList<EditorToolDefinition> Tools;
+
+    /// <summary>Loads the embedded catalog once and compiles its input schemas with JsonSchema.Net.</summary>
+    static EditorToolCatalog()
+    {
+        Assembly Assembly = typeof(EditorToolCatalog).Assembly;
+        string ResourceName = Assembly.GetManifestResourceNames()
+            .Single(name => name.EndsWith(".editor-tools.json", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+
+        using Stream Resource = Assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(ResourceName);
+        using JsonDocument Catalog = JsonDocument.Parse(Resource);
+        List<EditorToolDefinition> Definitions = new();
+        Dictionary<string, JsonSchema> Schemas = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+        foreach (JsonElement Element in Catalog.RootElement.GetProperty("tools").EnumerateArray())
+        {
+            EditorToolDefinition Definition = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<EditorToolDefinition>(Element.GetRawText());
+            Definition.InputSchema = Definition.InputSchema.Clone();
+            Definition.OutputSchema = Definition.OutputSchema.Clone();
+            Definitions.Add(Definition);
+            Schemas.Add(Definition.Name, JsonSchema.Build(Definition.InputSchema));
+            JsonSchema.Build(Definition.OutputSchema);
+        }
+
+        Tools = new ReadOnlyCollection<EditorToolDefinition>(Definitions);
+        InputSchemas = new ReadOnlyDictionary<string, JsonSchema>(Schemas);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Returns all 107 tool definitions in the order declared by the embedded contract.</summary>
+    /// <returns>A read-only list with input/output schemas and conservative ownership metadata.</returns>
+    public static IReadOnlyList<EditorToolDefinition> GetTools()
+    {
+        return Tools;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Evaluates one tool argument object against its compiled JSON Schema.</summary>
+    /// <param name="ToolName">The case-sensitive tool name registered in the catalog.</param>
+    /// <param name="Arguments">The candidate JSON value supplied by a client.</param>
+    /// <param name="Errors">A read-only collection explaining schema errors or an unknown tool.</param>
+    /// <returns>True only when the tool exists and JsonSchema.Net accepts the complete input.</returns>
+    public static bool TryValidateInputArguments(string ToolName, JsonElement Arguments, out IReadOnlyList<string> Errors)
+    {
+        if (!InputSchemas.TryGetValue(ToolName, out JsonSchema Schema))
+        {
+            Errors = Array.AsReadOnly(new[] { $"Unknown editor tool '{ToolName}'." });
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        return TryValidate(Schema, Arguments, out Errors);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Validates a value against a caller-supplied JSON Schema, including dynamic document patch schemas.</summary>
+    /// <param name="SchemaDocument">A complete JSON Schema document returned by an editor schema query.</param>
+    /// <param name="Candidate">The JSON value to check against the supplied schema.</param>
+    /// <param name="Errors">A read-only collection describing schema rejection, or an empty collection on success.</param>
+    /// <returns>True only when JsonSchema.Net accepts the candidate instance.</returns>
+    public static bool TryValidateAgainstSchema(JsonElement SchemaDocument, JsonElement Candidate, out IReadOnlyList<string> Errors)
+    {
+        JsonSchema Schema = JsonSchema.Build(SchemaDocument);
+        return TryValidate(Schema, Candidate, out Errors);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Evaluates a compiled JSON Schema and returns a stable non-empty rejection explanation.</summary>
+    /// <param name="Schema">The compiled schema used for validation.</param>
+    /// <param name="Candidate">The JSON value being evaluated.</param>
+    /// <param name="Errors">A read-only collection with a generic schema failure explanation.</param>
+    /// <returns>True when the compiled schema accepts the candidate instance.</returns>
+    static bool TryValidate(JsonSchema Schema, JsonElement Candidate, out IReadOnlyList<string> Errors)
+    {
+        EvaluationResults Results = Schema.Evaluate(Candidate, new EvaluationOptions());
+        if (Results.IsValid)
+        {
+            Errors = Array.Empty<string>();
+            return true;
+        }
+
+        Errors = Array.AsReadOnly(new[] { "The JSON value does not satisfy its JSON Schema." });
+        return false;
+    }
+}

@@ -0,0 +1,46 @@
+using System.Text.Json;
+using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
+
+namespace helengine.editor.automation;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Publishes the schema and conservative behavioral metadata for one editor tool.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class EditorToolDefinition
+{
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the unique MCP-facing operation name.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("name")]
+    public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the JSON Schema used to validate tool arguments before dispatch.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("input_schema")]
+    public JsonElement InputSchema { get; set; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the explicit success-or-error result schema for the operation.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("output_schema")]
+    public JsonElement OutputSchema { get; set; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets whether this operation may change editor or project state.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("mutates")]
+    public bool Mutates { get; set; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets whether undo is guaranteed for every successful mutation of this tool.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("undoable")]
+    public bool Undoable { get; set; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the declared persistence behavior for the operation's effects.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("persistence")]
+    public string Persistence { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the task number that owns implementation of the operation.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("owner_task")]
+    public int OwnerTask { get; set; }
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the audited editor source that provides this operation's behavior.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("functional_source")]
+    public string FunctionalSource { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+
+    /// <summary>Gets or sets the behavior-test identity required before this tool is implemented.</summary>
+    [JsonPropertyName("behavior_test")]
+    public string BehaviorTest { get; set; } = string.Empty;
+}

index 00000000..c6a315bd

+using System.Reflection;
+using System.Text.Json;
+using helengine.editor.automation;
+
+namespace helengine.editor.mcp.tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Checks the shared editor tool catalog's cross-tool contract requirements.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class EditorToolCatalogTests
+{
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Verifies every editor operation requires an explicit binding in its input schema.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Catalog_requires_binding_for_editor_tools()
+    {
+        Type catalogType = GetCatalogType();
+
+        MethodInfo getToolsMethod = catalogType.GetMethod("GetTools", BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static, Type.EmptyTypes);
+        Assert.NotNull(getToolsMethod);
+
+        object definitions = getToolsMethod.Invoke(null, null);
+        Assert.NotNull(definitions);
+
+        List<string> missingBindings = new();
+        foreach (object definition in (IEnumerable<object>)definitions)
+        {
+            string name = (string)definition.GetType().GetProperty("Name").GetValue(definition);
+            if (name == "list_editors" || name == "bind_editor")
+            {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            JsonElement inputSchema = (JsonElement)definition.GetType().GetProperty("InputSchema").GetValue(definition);
+            bool hasBinding = inputSchema.GetProperty("required").EnumerateArray()
+                .Any(field => field.GetString() == "binding_id");
+            if (!hasBinding)
+            {
+                missingBindings.Add(name);
+            }
+        }
+
+        Assert.Empty(missingBindings);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Verifies the catalog contains every planned operation and reports required ownership metadata.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Catalog_declares_all_operations_and_metadata_without_claiming_implementation()
+    {
+        Type catalogType = GetCatalogType();
+        MethodInfo getToolsMethod = catalogType.GetMethod("GetTools", BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static, Type.EmptyTypes);
+        Assert.NotNull(getToolsMethod);
+        IEnumerable<object> definitions = (IEnumerable<object>)getToolsMethod.Invoke(null, null);
+
+        List<object> definitionList = definitions.ToList();
+        Assert.Equal(107, definitionList.Count);
+        Assert.Equal(107, definitionList.Select(definition => (string)definition.GetType().GetProperty("Name").GetValue(definition)).Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Count());
+
+        foreach (object definition in definitionList)
+        {
+            Type definitionType = definition.GetType();
+            string name = (string)definitionType.GetProperty("Name").GetValue(definition);
+            JsonElement inputSchema = (JsonElement)definitionType.GetProperty("InputSchema").GetValue(definition);
+            JsonElement outputSchema = (JsonElement)definitionType.GetProperty("OutputSchema").GetValue(definition);
+            Assert.False(inputSchema.GetProperty("additionalProperties").GetBoolean());
+            Assert.Equal(2, outputSchema.GetProperty("oneOf").GetArrayLength());
+            Assert.True((int)definitionType.GetProperty("OwnerTask").GetValue(definition) > 0);
+            Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace((string)definitionType.GetProperty("FunctionalSource").GetValue(definition)));
+            Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace((string)definitionType.GetProperty("BehaviorTest").GetValue(definition)));
+            Assert.Contains((string)definitionType.GetProperty("Persistence").GetValue(definition), new[] { "none", "scene_dirty", "immediate_disk", "job_output", "type_defined", "command_defined" });
+
+            List<string> required = inputSchema.GetProperty("required").EnumerateArray().Select(field => field.GetString()).ToList();
+            bool mutates = (bool)definitionType.GetProperty("Mutates").GetValue(definition);
+            if (mutates && name != "bind_editor" && name != "unbind_editor")
+            {
+                Assert.Contains("expected_revision", required);
+                Assert.Contains("operation_id", required);
+            }
+
+            if (new[] { "scenes_list", "entities_list", "component_types_list", "components_list", "assets_list", "asset_dependencies_get", "jobs_list", "logs_read", "project_commands_list" }.Contains(name, StringComparer.Ordinal))
+            {
+                JsonElement limit = inputSchema.GetProperty("properties").GetProperty("limit");
+                Assert.Equal(1, limit.GetProperty("minimum").GetInt32());
+                Assert.Equal(500, limit.GetProperty("maximum").GetInt32());
+                Assert.Equal(100, limit.GetProperty("default").GetInt32());
+            }
+
+            Assert.DoesNotContain("implemented", string.Join(" ", definitionType.GetProperties().Select(property => property.Name)), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Verifies strict schemas reject unknown properties, wrong types, and invalid discriminated objects.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Schemas_reject_unknown_and_wrong_typed_fields()
+    {
+        bool unknownFieldAccepted = ValidateInput("list_editors", """{"unexpected":true}""");
+        bool wrongTypedFieldAccepted = ValidateInput("list_editors", """{"project_root":false}""");
+        bool invalidScopeAccepted = ValidateInput("entity_update", """{"binding_id":"b","scene_handle":"s","expected_revision":"1","operation_id":"op","entity_id":"e","scope":{"kind":"environment","platform_id":"desktop"},"patch":{"name":"Renamed"}}""");
+        bool emptyReferenceAccepted = ValidateInput("asset_reference_resolve", """{"binding_id":"b","reference":{},"expected_kind":"mesh"}""");
+        bool invalidPayloadAccepted = unknownFieldAccepted || wrongTypedFieldAccepted || invalidScopeAccepted || emptyReferenceAccepted;
+
+        Assert.False(invalidPayloadAccepted);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Verifies a correctly discriminated environment scope passes the same runtime schema validator.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Schemas_accept_valid_composite_scope()
+    {
+        bool validPayloadAccepted = ValidateInput("entity_update", """{"binding_id":"b","scene_handle":"s","expected_revision":"1","operation_id":"op","entity_id":"e","scope":{"kind":"environment","platform_id":"desktop","environment_id":"debug"},"patch":{"name":"Renamed"}}""");
+
+        Assert.True(validPayloadAccepted);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Verifies the shared runtime API enforces a fetched type-specific patch schema with JsonSchema.Net.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Schemas_validate_dynamic_patch_fields_with_the_json_schema_library()
+    {
+        using JsonDocument schema = JsonDocument.Parse("""{"$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema","type":"object","properties":{"name":{"type":"string"}},"required":["name"],"additionalProperties":false}""");
+        using JsonDocument validPatch = JsonDocument.Parse("""{"name":"Renamed"}""");
+        using JsonDocument wrongTypePatch = JsonDocument.Parse("""{"name":42}""");
+        using JsonDocument unknownFieldPatch = JsonDocument.Parse("""{"name":"Renamed","unknown":true}""");
+
+        bool validPatchAccepted = EditorToolCatalog.TryValidateAgainstSchema(schema.RootElement, validPatch.RootElement, out IReadOnlyList<string> validErrors);
+        bool wrongTypeAccepted = EditorToolCatalog.TryValidateAgainstSchema(schema.RootElement, wrongTypePatch.RootElement, out IReadOnlyList<string> wrongTypeErrors);
+        bool unknownFieldAccepted = EditorToolCatalog.TryValidateAgainstSchema(schema.RootElement, unknownFieldPatch.RootElement, out IReadOnlyList<string> unknownFieldErrors);
+
+        Assert.True(validPatchAccepted);
+        Assert.Empty(validErrors);
+        Assert.False(wrongTypeAccepted);
+        Assert.NotEmpty(wrongTypeErrors);
+        Assert.False(unknownFieldAccepted);
+        Assert.NotEmpty(unknownFieldErrors);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Verifies the shared defaults match the bounded contract and can be reduced in tests.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Automation_limits_expose_contract_defaults_and_validate_injected_bounds()
+    {
+        EditorAutomationLimits defaults = new();
+        Assert.Equal(100, defaults.DefaultPageSize);
+        Assert.Equal(500, defaults.MaximumPageSize);
+        Assert.Equal(1024, defaults.MaximumPendingCalls);
+        Assert.Equal(64, defaults.MaximumBindings);
+        Assert.Equal(10000, defaults.MaximumOperationIdsPerBinding);
+        Assert.Equal(4 * 1024 * 1024, defaults.MaximumRequestBytes);
+        Assert.Equal(10000, defaults.MaximumRetainedLogEntries);
+
+        EditorAutomationLimits injected = new(3, 8, 10, 2, 20, 1024, 30);
+        Assert.Equal(3, injected.DefaultPageSize);
+        Assert.Equal(8, injected.MaximumPageSize);
+        Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => new EditorAutomationLimits(9, 8, 10, 2, 20, 1024, 30));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Verifies contract DTO JSON field names remain snake_case at the serialization boundary.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Contract_dtos_serialize_with_snake_case_property_names()
+    {
+        using JsonDocument arguments = JsonDocument.Parse("{}");
+        EditorOperationRequest request = new() { Arguments = arguments.RootElement.Clone() };
+        using JsonDocument document = JsonDocument.Parse(JsonSerializer.Serialize(request));
+        Assert.True(document.RootElement.TryGetProperty("tool_name", out _));
+        Assert.True(document.RootElement.TryGetProperty("binding_id", out _));
+        Assert.True(document.RootElement.TryGetProperty("expected_revision", out _));
+        Assert.False(document.RootElement.TryGetProperty("ToolName", out _));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Gets the shared catalog type without requiring it to exist during the red test phase.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <returns>The loaded editor automation catalog type.</returns>
+    static Type GetCatalogType()
+    {
+        Type catalogType = Type.GetType("helengine.editor.automation.EditorToolCatalog, helengine.editor.automation");
+        Assert.NotNull(catalogType);
+        return catalogType;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Runs the catalog's public JSON Schema validation API against a test payload.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="ToolName">The name of the tool whose input schema should be evaluated.</param>
+    /// <param name="Payload">The JSON payload to validate.</param>
+    /// <returns>True when the catalog accepts the payload.</returns>
+    static bool ValidateInput(string ToolName, string Payload)
+    {
+        MethodInfo validateMethod = GetCatalogType().GetMethod("TryValidateInputArguments", BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static);
+        Assert.NotNull(validateMethod);
+
+        using JsonDocument document = JsonDocument.Parse(Payload);
+        object[] arguments = { ToolName, document.RootElement, null };
+        return (bool)validateMethod.Invoke(null, arguments);
+    }
+}

+using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
+using Microsoft.Extensions.Hosting;
+using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
+
+namespace helengine.editor.mcp.tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Starts the SDK's stdio server when this test assembly is launched as a child process.
+/// </summary>
+public static class McpSdkTestHost
+{
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Runs only for the stdio child-process mode used by the SDK compatibility test.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="Arguments">The command-line arguments supplied to the process.</param>
+    /// <returns>Zero after the stdio host shuts down.</returns>
+    public static async Task<int> Main(string[] Arguments)
+    {
+        if (Arguments.Length != 1 || Arguments[0] != "--stdio-server")
+        {
+            return 2;
+        }
+
+        Console.Error.WriteLine($"STDIO_RUNTIME={Environment.Version}");
+        HostApplicationBuilder builder = Host.CreateApplicationBuilder(Array.Empty<string>());
+        builder.Logging.ClearProviders();
+        builder.Services.AddMcpServer()
+            .WithStdioServerTransport()
+            .WithTools<McpTestTools>();
+
+        await builder.Build().RunAsync();
+        return 0;
+    }
+}
diff --git a/engine/helengine.editor.mcp.tests/McpTestHttpServer.cs b/engine/helengine.editor.mcp.tests/McpTestHttpServer.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..2d9c27db
--- /dev/null
+++ b/engine/helengine.editor.mcp.tests/McpTestHttpServer.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,83 @@
+using System.Net;
+using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Builder;
+using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Hosting;
+using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Hosting.Server;
+using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Hosting.Server.Features;
+using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
+using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
+using ModelContextProtocol.AspNetCore;
+
+namespace helengine.editor.mcp.tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Runs a real loopback Kestrel host for the SDK's Streamable HTTP client test.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class McpTestHttpServer : IAsyncDisposable
+{
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Creates a server wrapper for its running ASP.NET application and endpoint.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="Application">The running ASP.NET Core application.</param>
+    /// <param name="Endpoint">The assigned Streamable HTTP endpoint.</param>
+    McpTestHttpServer(WebApplication Application, Uri Endpoint)
+    {
+        this.Application = Application;
+        this.Endpoint = Endpoint;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Gets the assigned MCP route on the loopback listener.
+    /// </summary>
+    public Uri Endpoint { get; }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Holds the running application until the test disposes this wrapper.
+    /// </summary>
+    readonly WebApplication Application;
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Starts Kestrel on an ephemeral loopback port and maps the stateless MCP route.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <returns>A running HTTP test server with its actual bound endpoint.</returns>
+    public static async Task<McpTestHttpServer> StartAsync()
+    {
+        WebApplicationBuilder builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(new WebApplicationOptions
+        {
+            Args = Array.Empty<string>()
+        });
+        builder.Logging.ClearProviders();
+        builder.WebHost.ConfigureKestrel(options => options.Listen(IPAddress.Loopback, 0));
+        builder.Services.AddMcpServer()
+            .WithHttpTransport(options => options.SessionMode = HttpServerSessionMode.Stateless)
+            .WithTools<McpTestTools>();
+
+        WebApplication application = builder.Build();
+        application.MapMcp("/mcp");
+        await application.StartAsync();
+
+        try
+        {
+            IServerAddressesFeature addresses = application.Services
+                .GetRequiredService<IServer>()
+                .Features
+                .Get<IServerAddressesFeature>();
+            string address = addresses.Addresses.Single();
+            Uri endpoint = new(new Uri(address), "/mcp");
+            return new McpTestHttpServer(application, endpoint);
+        }
+        catch
+        {
+            await application.DisposeAsync();
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Stops Kestrel and releases the server's resources.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <returns>A task that completes after application disposal.</returns>
+    public ValueTask DisposeAsync()
+    {
+        return Application.DisposeAsync();
+    }
+}

new file mode 100644
index 00000000..65255928

+using ModelContextProtocol.Client;
+using ModelContextProtocol.Protocol;
+using System.Collections.Concurrent;
+using Xunit.Abstractions;
+
+namespace helengine.editor.mcp.tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Proves SDK 2.2.0 can exchange tool calls over both required transports on .NET 9.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class SdkCompatibilityTests
+{
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Captures negotiated SDK and protocol versions for the test report.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="Output">The test output writer.</param>
+    public SdkCompatibilityTests(ITestOutputHelper Output)
+    {
+        this.Output = Output;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Holds the xUnit output writer for protocol and runtime version evidence.
+    /// </summary>
+    readonly ITestOutputHelper Output;
+
+    /// <summary>Collects only the child server's stderr callback, leaving stdout on the MCP protocol pipe.</summary>
+    readonly ConcurrentQueue<string> StandardErrorLines = new();
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Exchanges a real ping tool call over stateless HTTP and child-process stdio.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <returns>A task that completes when both transports return the expected response.</returns>
+    [Fact]
+    public async Task Sdk_can_exchange_tool_calls_over_stdio_and_http_on_net9()
+    {
+        Assert.Equal(9, Environment.Version.Major);
+
+        await using McpTestHttpServer httpServer = await McpTestHttpServer.StartAsync();
+        HttpClientTransport httpTransport = new(new HttpClientTransportOptions
+        {
+            Endpoint = httpServer.Endpoint,
+            TransportMode = HttpTransportMode.StreamableHttp,
+            ConnectionTimeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10)
+        });
+        await using McpClient httpClient = await McpClient.CreateAsync(httpTransport);
+        string httpProtocolVersion = httpClient.NegotiatedProtocolVersion;
+        CallToolResult httpToolResult = await httpClient.CallToolAsync("ping");
+        string httpResult = Assert.IsType<TextContentBlock>(Assert.Single(httpToolResult.Content)).Text;
+        Assert.Equal("pong", httpResult);
+        Output.WriteLine($"HTTP: ModelContextProtocol.AspNetCore 2.2.0; protocol {httpProtocolVersion}; runtime {Environment.Version}.");
+
+        StdioClientTransport stdioTransport = new(new StdioClientTransportOptions
+        {
+            Command = "dotnet",
+            Arguments = new[] { typeof(McpSdkTestHost).Assembly.Location, "--stdio-server" },
+            WorkingDirectory = AppContext.BaseDirectory,
+            ShutdownTimeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10),
+            StandardErrorLines = line => this.StandardErrorLines.Enqueue(line)
+        });
+        await using McpClient stdioClient = await McpClient.CreateAsync(stdioTransport);
+        string stdioProtocolVersion = stdioClient.NegotiatedProtocolVersion;
+        CallToolResult stdioToolResult = await stdioClient.CallToolAsync("ping");
+        string stdioResult = Assert.IsType<TextContentBlock>(Assert.Single(stdioToolResult.Content)).Text;
+        Assert.Equal("pong", stdioResult);
+
+        string runtimeDiagnostic = this.StandardErrorLines.FirstOrDefault(line => line.StartsWith("STDIO_RUNTIME=", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+        DateTimeOffset diagnosticDeadline = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow.AddSeconds(5);
+        while (runtimeDiagnostic == null && DateTimeOffset.UtcNow < diagnosticDeadline)
+        {
+            await Task.Delay(20);
+            runtimeDiagnostic = this.StandardErrorLines.FirstOrDefault(line => line.StartsWith("STDIO_RUNTIME=", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+        }
+
+        Assert.NotNull(runtimeDiagnostic);
+        Assert.StartsWith("STDIO_RUNTIME=9.", runtimeDiagnostic);
+        Output.WriteLine($"stdio: ModelContextProtocol 2.2.0; protocol {stdioProtocolVersion}; host diagnostic {runtimeDiagnostic} captured through stderr. The successful ping confirms stdout remained valid MCP JSON-RPC.");
+    }
+}
